35 Fuel Filter
36 Turbo Charger
37 Intake Duct
38 Gasket Replace
39 Intake Manifold

### Tools Required

000 589 77 03 00 Box Wrench Insert
001 589 65 09 00 Socket Wrench Insert
102 589 03 40 00 Magnetic Bar
116 589 02 34 00 Threaded Pin
116 589 03 07 00 T Type Socket Wrench
116 589 20 33 00 Sliding Hammer
601 589 00 10 00 Cylinder Head Bolt Wrench
602 589 00 40 00 Engine Lock
603 589 00 40 00 Counter Holder

### Notice

The cylinder head may only be removed when the engine has cooled down. The cylinder head is removed together with the exhaust manifold. As the cylinder head bolts undergo a permanent tightening. They require to be replaced if they exceed the maximum lengthes indicated in the table.

![](images/58b755625c42b0822d6129cc19142b20cb3196cfb532f8d6805e12e1a7fa5096.jpg)

The twelve-sided socket head bolts are tightened with each stages of torque and torque angle.

It is not necessary to retighten the cylinder head bolts at the 1000\~1500km inspection or after 1000\~1500km of repairs.

![](images/e2f4d79dbc6547689781f1af427dc8db328226d10e77866a18b4bb0441044eb1.jpg)

### Disassembly Procedure

1. Completely drain the coolant from the radiator and cylinder block.
2. Remove the cooling fan shroud.
3. Hold the fan with counter holder and remove the bolt and then remove the cooling fan.

### Notice

Keep the fan in vertical position.

Counter Holder 603 589 00 40 00

4. Remove the bracket oil dipstick tube.

![](images/8fe8de4c61da1c3db1d829a3103de3d6a268e952dbfd61eab1c777aae2dab5ed.jpg)

![](images/0427fed3d8bcccbf04fcaed852619a70206339b49c3384761339e6ad71fed72b.jpg)

5. Remove the nut.
6. Remove the nut on the tensioning lever and insert the rod(F12 180mm). By pushing the rod to the arrow direction, pull back the bolt.

7. Push the tensioning lever to the opposite direction to release the spring tension and remove the poly V-belt.

![](images/8248b26a7f1dbdaab53f54bb298ea1a85c0df8e9f7acdef6a5731a5e411176a6.jpg)

8. Remove the air cleaner cover and element and then remove the air cleaner housing. 9. Remove the oil return hose and plug.

### Notice

Cover them to prevent chips from coming into.

![](images/9b351bfccd2edf012c3e91237eaf1bcefb85046a8440c5f1c87eed337cd7a764.jpg)

10. Unscrew the EGR pipe mounting bolts onto the exhaust manifold. 11. Remove the duct bracket from the cylinder head.

![](images/581a8f7a98be18e2a359a43a0a376744015b8dde98ebf1eed5513fe926adf49c.jpg)

12. Unscrew the intake duct mounting bolts onto the intake manifold.

![](images/a872e68cfc60d554057b67467c156dc37a6f9506b3a248ed8f9f9d0481015098.jpg)

13. Separate the connecting rod from the control lever.

![](images/c8b0a96eeec47af6749e52ee87f5fb6936d71eef964319bfe0dceab93e0d65aa.jpg)

14. Pull out the accelerator control linkage.

![](images/b566c2f3e1d8bd87f4ebad040d6d9ccadc51027d4a33354ab4b349fdb80651b5.jpg)

15. Remove the fuel injection line(1) from the fuel injection nozzle(12).

Box Wrench Inset 000 589 77 03 00

![](images/3da28c64da7c5f551780ff7edeede9151c943655f01d5ae4c4fba55001fc9d4e.jpg)

16. Remove the fuel injection line from the fuel injection pump.

Box Wrench Insert 000 589 77 03 00

![](images/1bcd67033db6db3f2a063c81ddb3818eba094599fb2830749a7fd7b67bc71262.jpg)

17. Remove the bracket mounting bolts and then remove the fuel injection line(1).

![](images/090d096ab241186dae6e5cd2814b6e72bced82935566016f3822ba93e10bb96f.jpg)

18. Disconnect the booster hose connected to intake manifold.

19. Remove the intake manifold and gasket.

![](images/5bab4e739804b996584f08f0fba73a137fafeb83193c7301ee8e75f8905fe101.jpg)

20. Remove the cylinder head cover and gasket with the blowby gas hose.

21. Disconnect the glow plug cables.

![](images/bbd93fe4e8ed9cb818553aed348e9b61d85e8aed7056c1d281ca9442a4d42c00.jpg)

22. Remove the cable channel.

![](images/faabc3582fb2960f01d5fd4bf8b0c46e1fc53ba54797daca7c0c3e2bc3759df8.jpg)

23. Disconnect the cables from the glow plug sensor and coolant temperature sensor.

24. Remove the heater pipe bracket from the oil filter.

![](images/3476a36e1f7d6b8432ffdb35dbf40860e9754464dda1b1b1743d151ad2cc6ac6.jpg)

25. Pry off the clamp and push the heater feed pipe forward and then pull out the pipe.

26. Disconnect the fuel lines from the fuel filter.

![](images/df66145eea9fbd552ff4d56764bf9e0595798a8cf542b958de553dc831adc175.jpg)

27. Disconnect the fuel lines from the injection pump.

![](images/ea3e47d8bc3b7a35f5fc24d497c3474ff6c90c665d5570ef58b52c3a685ccf1a.jpg)

28. Remove the fuel filter(35).

![](images/4aa843927292614943007bfb41057f1b18def92db342979656b9a554309f020b.jpg)

29. Remove the fuel injection nozzle(12) and nozzle washer(11).

Socket Wrench Insert 001 589 65 09 00

30. Rotate the crankshaft and set the no.1 cylinder at TDC.

### Notice

Do not rotate the crankshaft to the opposite direction of engine revolution.

![](images/f6629cf053d5257d1d52765f91acd5fc722e0e30b935e4fc593d1d7afda158d0.jpg)

31. Place alignment marks on the camshaft gear and timing chain.

![](images/8698bd509a2b4ac7229eb6b6347d4602de5afcb6ddbdc2cf3a4e6fc1ea9f4c5a.jpg)

32. Ensure that the camshaft and the bearing cap marking are aligned.

![](images/b4ea123c5f2f5c4892c5e03dc8404d7245eb751764fd0931c395919b5bcb476c.jpg)

33. Remove the starter motor and install the engine lock onto the flywheel ring gear.

Engine Lock 602 589 00 40 00

![](images/842831a7ae7ff3226bd4f75f61866f1e6b7651d70018ada269dec85f14576157.jpg)

34. Remove the turbocharger.

![](images/1884515e330cdc82d8b507eca831b30f8c4ae2bbbdceea735bb499ba27cfbf16.jpg)

35. Remove the exhaust manifold and gasket.

36. Remove the chain tensioner and seal.

![](images/ef7fdf9461202fbaec3e5f8d608474282bb0fa75ca50b4f563fe9cf8c6a6f987.jpg)

37. Remove the bolt and separate the drive sprocket(21).

### Notice

During removal, be careful not to drop the sprocket and chain into the timing case. Carefully pull off the chain and then pull out the sprocket.

38. Remove the camshaft bearing cap bolts according to the numerical sequence.

### Notice

Remove the No.1 bolts first and then remove the No. 2 bolts. Do not remove the bolts at a time completely but remove them step by step evenly or camshaft can be seriously damaged.

OM662LA

![](images/d77ffbf32125b4c61f5b0472ea9729159d4586346bdd643d65719dd22dcbb8ab.jpg)

39. Remove the bearing caps and then pull out the camshaft(20) upward.

### Notice

Be careful not to miss the locking washer.

![](images/3ff55f4e78588536844a544ed007a85c6e76e2a61802ab10bdec5182dfe0fdb1.jpg)

40. Remove the locking washer.

### Notice

Check the locking washer and replace if necessary.

![](images/3c1b4e3d08151a0cd4c2bcd7ca223d111f48a6c910d5aa4feced675f9981516a.jpg)

41. Remove the bolt(32).

![](images/f532b76648eff297f37b625905cdc6c9fdb9eaf2bbebc50e7189479cd646f26c.jpg)

42. Separate the spring and pull out the tensioning lever(31).

![](images/0e379dff0f0a386f64f1bc382d3cd357621e3b31febfb3ed0079f8a34975338c.jpg)

43. Pry off the closing cover. Remove the bolt and then remove the idle pulley.

![](images/cd9c903ef31c286f8fcbf3a47f14102acc32077a0775b6734796d0d681e37609.jpg)

44. Using the sliding hammer(36) and the threaded pin(37), pull out the sliding rail pins(25, 26) and remove the sliding rail(24).

Sliding Hammer 116 589 20 33 00
Threaded Pin 116 589 02 34 00

![](images/4decaa08338642ed9475242ef4a9ce960260b523cede56a9b36472cd11ad9d26.jpg)

45. Remove the vacuum line from the vacuum pump.

![](images/a685edb0d16d9002be0eff3b4f21e83bc23f32d8a634b94dddd089e600e53284.jpg)

46. Disconnect the vacuum pipe from thermo valve.

![](images/f9bbd081738f0642fe1cae1cdcde2ecb01678ad28d330269d0f6b4adc07b2580.jpg)

47. Remove the socket bolts(13) of the chain box.

T Type Socket Wrench 116 589 03 07 00
Magnetic Bar 102 589 03 40 00

![](images/0a5d5615dfc21d09db5134384dfc173a181411c2eb67b72e37f287737b6d206b.jpg)

48. Remove the cylinder head bolts in numerical se-quence.

Cylinder Head Bolt Wrench 601 589 00 10 00

OM 662LA

![](images/9127d572f5590285ce1a8d5e0a0adeea32a5c6a1e2e07c3a2d257e3a37f28a80.jpg)

OM 661LA

![](images/df737a6a4fc3df39593d2ca2b5d83fb0c80c8cacea739144ed38b2cb2be5f461.jpg)

49. Remove the cylinder head(2) and gasket(3).

![](images/2351c524dc1a2547a2ba1025c129661cebf218055fb5de324dd2771c733b9448.jpg)

### Assembly Procedure

1. Replace the cylinder head gasket.

![](images/17fe2a7de07fedb924fa7e56629555a3ce9afe3b6090d7cc6f39c5f35d1fad45.jpg)

2. Install the cylinder head onto the crankcase.

### Notice

Align the cylinder head holes with the guide pins.

![](images/d9a4f5e60abbbad019c08da9a25063404aa198e83f5fd5facf7c637a8be42e58.jpg)

3. Measure the length(L) of cylinder head bolts. Notice If the max. length is exceeded, replace the bolts.

![](images/b62d0864eadd7417a1b6ca659a167f5edc8a595a847f464848592cc0f6b528d3.jpg)

![](images/20e36cd0fd14408e0cb58bb59edd217f20fb8ede071755c977ded58abcaab8a3.jpg)

4. Coat the head contact surface of bolts and thread with oil and insert them as shown.

Cylinder head bolts arrangement Bore 1 M10 × 80 2 M10 × 102 3 M10 × 115 4 M 8 × 50 5 M 8 × 80

![](images/06393e2366dc994d1b7c1a93b03108840113af63a72619a4eecdeb2544f1c95d.jpg)

5. Tighten the cylinder head bolts to specified torque and torque angle.

![](images/48d04e456c3dbebdedaa2f9c6c089ca0606204b38e012504360bae62d97179d6.jpg)

![](images/f910e14f71b1bdd800ec6657331e1c08a3d17f06bc492e2cb5d808bc0f9809a4.jpg)

### OM 662LA

![](images/d817cd6eba514c6925d745ef7904f5a422b10144dd6afee88bd3671e69bef748.jpg)

6. Install the socket bolts in the chain box.

![](images/0c0d669b05734231577fd6bc0f7cb3bd00b0f1a29bf3871ef1fa3f3a6b576b68.jpg)

![](images/03295410567fc1539da1ae22b6984ae576144dace135333e4be4b06c6b62b1cf.jpg)

7. Connect the vacuum pipe to the thermo valve.

![](images/fc8be8376031edd1a87e66d834d1f8d7e89955f954e7da6e8291589094555766.jpg)

8. Connect the vacuum lines to the vacuum pump.

![](images/921e3c28224f264a0edee21901a14e59c589be76f62e46772c408d47245eae1c.jpg)

9. Install the fuel filter and connect the pipe.

![](images/89f44ad191eb566c59920cb87e47a886efebdfcef20d68daf117e902f9dd7ab1.jpg)

### Notice

Be careful not to be confused the connections and hoses.

![](images/df65987aad350c7d0cce8b7b7e9ee455f905b456aaa454a1fe79c5854f322865.jpg)

10. Connect the fuel pipe to the injection pump.

![](images/acd722785756d2626d178b201b5deeb0388beba4fd0b25cf6bb41bfe867c6039.jpg)

11. Install the sliding rail(24) and insert the sliding rail pins(25, 26).

### Notice

Apply sealing compound on the each collar of the sliding rail pins.

Sliding Hammer 116 589 20 33 00
Threaded Pin 116 589 02 34 00

![](images/63e206d9e2d39905254c51780222b7d98e46a33aa6c210b81949ff8148abe08e.jpg)

12. Install the idle pulley and fit the closing cover.

![](images/4e65a8bdbe4cb1fc48961197931f25f8bc9d47ad43655dcf9b96d18f1c736a18.jpg)

![](images/96625aac4aa07202304a72527401eb6764a243378f8152dba95ff8344e12b4e1.jpg)

13. Insert the tensioning lever(31) and install the spring.

![](images/22a1b79dee7938d8796d6f54b8ce1471a537b4b0c9b35614e29488879670d1ea.jpg)

14. Install the damper.

![](images/75987d12853c977f7ee3581d5ac308dbe6994651cfad5b0a6610becda00b08b9.jpg)

Notice Insert the tensioning lever bolts onto the mounting hole.

![](images/649115c139148454f19c6b6221b303f9005042715ca7857e33047628fcc9ddee.jpg)

15. Insert the locking washer.
16. Inspect the valve tappet and check that the tappet moves smoothly.

![](images/2ab7cd6981fa245e5dca38a8fc9736039e2c5ad744e50ab1d15e481e6e527851.jpg)

17. Coat the camshaft with oil and install the camshaft on the cylinder head to be TDC mark(arrow) upward.

18. Measure the axial end play of the camshaft.

![](images/20a37e9a95ae88323f9c990b1550d20b0adb05774aad302cee52e3c8834a9d94.jpg)

### Notice

If out of standard, adjust it with the proper thickness of locking washer.

![](images/514539748bbbc90df8dcd119222d0a87427c0535544ea625815b07418f63850f.jpg)

19. Install the bearing caps on the camshaft according to the number on the caps.

![](images/422187483640949bffdd133aede20626994fbf108b07339a19c5ea44c2a42483.jpg)

20. Tighten the bearing cap bolts according to the numerical sequence.

![](images/80df66201db8d3dc3d01a5f985eaf5d6b5ef0cc3771273de00ae703287430f01.jpg)

### Notice

Tighten the No. 1 bolts(light arrow) first and then tighten the No. 2 bolts(dark arrow) stage by stage.

OM662LA

![](images/f578f22995c27ef604e4e55780f38acc997a33503b1b0598363f620f6d431c3e.jpg)

OM661LA

![](images/593aee356353f849180add01cfa226dcec9d60af98f3af52bb21f7b896f2a591.jpg)

21. Position the camshaft on marking and install the camshaft sprocket.

### Notice

Align the alignment marks on the chain and sprocket.

![](images/89506cbed0955e267a7f2c846566b85b9e08b4fc4b263a306c5a5ffb8fa235cf.jpg)

![](images/6003d9403592b7c87da579b9a7cbc6c680727e48ecad6e22a529a2309d65804d.jpg)

22. Check the TDC position of the crankshaft.

![](images/fe614ef456f1cf2be0946445a7468021664ad965ca3d9d66b7926b594b857e62.jpg)

23. Install the camshaft sprocket bolt.

![](images/db0179c95566c7e56ecf98064a9ba29da1d848b72669e2cb5763af277cb4e73a.jpg)

![](images/33c68a3ffabb7b8f725568b66ef98180f65bc74f6958838f3777e0e385723bf0.jpg)

### Notice

Measure the max. length ‘L’ and replace the bolt if it exceeds 53.6mm.

![](images/93b640ac8d2839d6be791e662a1afcf2b8e031807ef75cfb8086cbe385a73658.jpg)

24. Install the exhaust manifold and gasket.

![](images/bd2fb08f1e9ceca60a71a9373d46dd14fb0bac0320ace4361d7eaa611eef5dbd.jpg)

25. Install the turbocharger.

26. Install the exhaust pipe onto the turbocharger.

![](images/f41ff2c8930c141a25ed49e226a34f8e0ebb11941e05318f9518abaf4bcd0413.jpg)

![](images/9e33dda7103c25379d84252e2d2b7e3fbc86177c7b4b3f59ef4eca1f04e2af7a.jpg)

27. Replace the seal and then install the chain tensioner.

![](images/28bc604ea130f2c73712646feb2b53a66e403a528a597a18f5a135dd584fd5c9.jpg)

28. Remove the engine lock.

![](images/96b3d8ad8dbfe465048e8ac7d57888a66b2b20c28acb29561cb3504b664ff7b5.jpg)

29. Insert the nozzle washer into the hole to face round part downward.

30. Install the fuel injection nozzle.

![](images/6ba13f0a566be3ba2145d40e586b5cdaad1ac2d23c5e11d038371b2f33338295.jpg)

![](images/0da8a1307d553e33f77f7a591feb0506d5faaf67dc267b199df4195a27ef9bd6.jpg)

31. Connect the fuel hose.

32. Install the cable channel and connect the cables to glow plugs.

![](images/f38d3efb2262b7df35f80e61aa50d2f3ff788175367dd7247f03734801f5a7b4.jpg)

33. Replace the gasket and install the cylinder head cover.

![](images/c76e88945df90394cf398f88a3d132eb1d29098fe00927c34183894848df114e.jpg)

34. Install the blow-by hose.

![](images/046353182bf6e68e32ff79e38500398634adbd1efce368ce8f50c82f1b7509e6.jpg)

35. Connect the wires to the coolant temperature sensor and the glow plug sensor.

![](images/78038ec584834e2a257b807bf06060a1a8286b88cd9ed5ad4a0ae47ef9e6dfdd.jpg)

36. Replace the intake manifold gasket.

![](images/7dd4bb79bbc13b016fded9d46c8fe0e326b782ec6d0828d2349340cf8a53eb27.jpg)

37. Install the intake manifold.

![](images/1bb3ed091fbaa227e6539f67ba45714c64240adab178a999555aba2382151d0b.jpg)

![](images/fce43c3233390e985dcc71a69852541d8cf01c81c7c023916c2f11fa9a1289a5.jpg)

38. Replace the O-ring of heater feed pipe and install it to the cylinder head.

### Notice

For installation, clean the hole.

![](images/0bafff141220132b4d1dfeefbd5ea3c3024562854cab7236c9069b00d7aca26e.jpg)

39. Install the bracket of heater feed pipe to the oil filter.

![](images/f07562eb9882dae3b618c1c340c2b1f5f067b70bc7fdd3a806abeb260190248f.jpg)

40. Install the fuel pipe and the accelerator control linkage. 41. Connect the fuel lines to the injection nozzles and to the injection pump.

Box Wrench Insert 000 589 77 03 00

![](images/b3f9bc8e77887b51153969ca7ae8e659f0acfcc908e8d80b52fda07a696a73d0.jpg)

![](images/98db7ccf8e25950182323cfae0413d4b22a3a0f88b77782153f4d2332495d07b.jpg)

![](images/553e347e94140498b55e4aebce2e7b5812ed8c281f8cf12e53d5c6f8de82a232.jpg)

42. Install the poly V-belt.

### Notice

Be careful not to contaminate the belt.

![](images/8febb405688f4a7b531f955cbb9cff66995cfcab3429fbe45fdbd498851d5a4a.jpg)

43. By inserting a rod into the tensioning lever upper hole and pulling the rod, install the bolt and then tighten the nut.

![](images/1b50ebd7579c8436aa563fd7fcbbf31e0c24a1b871e7d383bc1ddac1b48872af.jpg)

![](images/7030d03f7466433f49b6d4cb3e8e2dc6a09432bcd42d3d23a3bd1851820aa004.jpg)

44. Install the oil dipstick tube bracket.

![](images/c94592cb808e57d6bdbb63c183dfeb41c17aa0224eb89d640bd3b8e9519859d2.jpg)

![](images/1bf0ebd5bf9f0675b1f83de7fbdf8d0d7f4d34f32ddc65fb8e2effcb62d49df9.jpg)

45. Hold the cooling fan with the counter holder and tighten the bolt.

![](images/f5f8540c009c9997330033f812a47a431eb0f10ace507b7746ae545e794f8d25.jpg)

### TIMING CASE COVER

Preceding Work : Removal of the cooling fan Rmoval of the V-belt tensioning device Removal of the vibration damper and hub Removal of the alternator

![](images/864c3787f75281cf4dce6d8b37d304a87412fae6d462a6bdd73d7822f93556ea.jpg)

1 Bolt . 10Nm
2 Cylinder Head Cover
3 Gasket Replace
4 Socket Bolt 25Nm
5 Fuel Filter
6 Square Nut
7 Oil Pan
8 Socket Bolt 10Nm
9 Bolt M6 : 10Nm
M8 23Nm
10 Power Steering Pump
11 Bolt
12 Belt Pulley
13 Bolt 32Nm
14 Timing Case Cover
15 Bolt 23Nm
16 Alternator Bracket
17 Bolt 45Nm
18 Bolt 25Nm
19 Bolt 10Nm
20 Closing Cover
21 Socket Bolt 23Nm
22 Guide Pulley
23 Bolt 9Nm
24 Guide Pulley Bracket
25 Nut 23Nm

### Tools Required

116 589 03 07 00 Socket Wrench

1. Remove the fan clutch and cooling fan belt pulley.
2. Drain the engine oil completely.
3. Remove the oil dipstick tube bracket bolts.
4. Remove the crankshaft pulley.
5. Loosen the bolt (1) and then remove the cylinder head cover
(2) and gasket.

6. Remove the vacuum pump.

### Removal Procedure

![](images/3352e3b5e0e07aaef36fbe7bfd56613eda991962a795a61d899b062e6834cbfe.jpg)

![](images/d22a795f74c629ad3c4fb75aa312fba40c16ad3731e74165845bfdd19d3d2371.jpg)

7. Detach the closing cover (20). Remove the bolts(21) and then remove the guide pulley (22).

8. Remove the guide pulley bracket (24).

![](images/d84cfb6e875b510aa0102a0b8f088a44d503324ea355f2d9c097d3f1e60e2892.jpg)

9. Disconnect the pipes of power steering pump and remove the belt pulley.

### Notice

Be careful not to lose the key.

![](images/0d93af54caa9fe0103e42dfeccf7a65f4a04a05b8874f5e604e24e7b7f395a87.jpg)

10. Remove the nut and pull out the bolt and then remove the power steering pump.

11. Remove the alternator bracket (16).

![](images/ea22b9a00072480f08a3ea7e7c538ea0a9677c7564ada7fece884208eb4011b9.jpg)

12. Remove the fuel filter.

13. Remove the camshaft.
14. Remove the socket bolts(4) in the chain box.

Socket Wrench 115 589 03 07 00

![](images/f947ab231c298d834e5e31ada3638a5163edb23dc3bf489b97646a62b6dd791e.jpg)

15. Remove the injection pump.

### Notice

See the ‘Removal of fuel injection pump’.

![](images/f2cefff6164fbfd8149c72cfea8fd516f79703573773773afcf1fbac435c29ef.jpg)

16. Remove the oil pan bolts (8, 9) in the area of the timing case cover (14). 17. Slightly loosen the remaining oil pan bolts.

18. Remove the timing case cover (19) bolts and then remove the timing case cover (14).

### Notice

Be careful not to damage the cylinder head gasket or oil pan gasket.

![](images/32b685b90930e6a8385f50f665add46f734a9943f4425beacca66becf40983be.jpg)

### Installation Procedure

1. Thoroughly clean the sealing surface and apply sealant.

![](images/f66077bf076a15a10700da24398f07c538d3f2b4767d85b5ed472e274e0eacf1.jpg)

2. Install the timing case cover.

![](images/4315c47ceeec3ef337c242a207a2d2e19dddbbdc4896f2945bef640508e323d0.jpg)

![](images/b93d26a66a773d554934b197a15946f0840bd71e883c54ce2abd719f4a36ff32.jpg)

### Notice

Bolts arrangement
1.M6 x 60
2.M6 x 70
3.M6 x 40

![](images/09ef349da41bd17d6b699ffe5a4547bbc58e9710f8784fc5c0a3877266ce9881.jpg)

3. Tighten the socket bolts in the chain box.

![](images/b4a5494e539ecc8327322b3f7236e9591734fd6f84277b29da09c1b60868aa3a.jpg)

4. Tighten the oil pan bolts.

![](images/2644933d4cc89e73cc75673200290989fdbd96e02ad4a843cadf3104c352c275.jpg)

![](images/5f29ccf555902c8d03b8e094de3d033709ac73bd432bdab20a7fbabb5438b3ad.jpg)

5. Install the flange, vibration damper and crankshaft belt pulley.

### Notice

Replace front radial seal if necessary.

![](images/b2afd7deb271f55625417575a1e1139a153e37153b085cf6f29b27a24b962afb.jpg)

6. Install the alternator bracket.

![](images/1945984c3d1c15c403031c88889daf38464aadde28507f66b645506fc8711f39.jpg)

![](images/e5b4ffde286f0247eecc6173ac46cc9026b1e7738d109b20749b59da4ce90494.jpg)

7. Install the alternator

![](images/3fbec2033a308914eb86b66b57e7dc1b918f1d30fbfd0db101a67f4a190ee862.jpg)

![](images/647fd5ec78cf2623d034dd14a9181280c5c95a6b0a949de662166c4fa5690395.jpg)

8. Install the cylinder head cover.

![](images/00c40a4d9e5daaa7e83d65a8deeb9d5e0e771d50c812eac01fe7a5bed1e32748.jpg)

9. Tighten the injection pump mounting bolts.

![](images/a16bcf1372691ffb269eac78b7063594656e26de6522339d2767dadeec7d913d.jpg)

![](images/cec65a4e88d7c653ac1da0b10d00f4e6d4ff4747a4065dd0f639d3ee76cf49e8.jpg)

10. Install the fuel filter.

![](images/7f9fe602795f2208b088cebc8ed607df62ec9c8169fe6c2762cd7d136813e671.jpg)

11. Install the vacuum pump.

![](images/23c9c025ab7d217d3c03c8b59a45f39e2c428682be0f81680272b98ff1af122c.jpg)

![](images/202956ee59b19a143474292a4dceb834a0fd1ef09a5036d8f032abe6e9c8583d.jpg)

12. Install the power steering pump.

![](images/ee0440c287fbe1aee734f9824afb4b862cecda9deba34785e2655c7e857588a3.jpg)

![](images/7cd8b80b462a9984298ec8a16bc9dcc55ff9179600e4b2c4b7ee3429188b4a7d.jpg)

13. Install the power steering pump pulley.

![](images/86d38bb0c5ab98615633918df8cb67a0e40836e4851f864b5c756497f85cfbd3.jpg)

![](images/fadd05d7357714c2c9d1b54f60ed034725d8d2e00e4bb8dda151302085f13310.jpg)

14. Install the guide pulley bracket.

![](images/02d1d5e789d2d5f4fb5c62622a45289b6f87e9642e08942a2709f6b4b9522cbf.jpg)

![](images/c3d35cf8b75095b4e0fafc3e31f01c240b473dd35c07ec46f4372f04a7a887fd.jpg)

15. Install the guide pulley (22) and fit the closing cover (20).

![](images/1a23a8ada9470e720426862ffdee21d2745308439d53fc95ff6bf1e613b33586.jpg)

![](images/41b8c301e1d9661f8df423031d97b71f3c083da9f1ae47db265abe11691639d0.jpg)

16. Replace the gasket (3) and install the cylinder head cover (2).

![](images/022de228c7dd82dd9a6216fe034ae676f7a87a2beabea2104ba1b38b630ed623.jpg)

17. Install the cooling fan belt pulley and fan clutch.

18. Install the belt tensioning device and then instal the belt.

19. Install the cooling fan.

20. Fill the engine oil and check oil leaks by running the engine.

![](images/edc81f579f67d0813effe2be8cce4f197efcffff90c7cb57dcc39a288b14b9b7.jpg)

### CRANKSHAFT END COVER

Preceding Work Removal of flywheel and driven plate.

![](images/e84485d0065458e7ea2590e181d43fb2289bfd931c3c69b00bfc7724707da897.jpg)

1 Crankshaft Flange
2 End Cover Clean, Loctite 573
3 Bolt 10Nm, Loctite 573

45 Radial Seal Replace Dowel Sleeve 6 Bolt 10Nm, Loctite 573

### Notice

Be careful not to damage the oil pan gasket.

![](images/9aebc7b786563dd70c6cdb31d2cf313ce106524038974839b5dbd4b914a569c4.jpg)

2. Remove the radial seal (4) with care not to damage the sealing surface.

![](images/2e773e79c7ecdc13b67efa8f4650bb756b27e1105d81402ebaaa15e7836ac852.jpg)

### Installation Procedure

1. Thoroughly clean the sealing surface of end cover and apply Loctite 573.

2. Clean the groove of radial seal.

3. Apply Loctite 573 on the bolts and install the end cover.

![](images/64d850ab57ee8e29f260c009b40e432149173a3dc58772fb296f56fca3cf52f2.jpg)

### Notice

Be careful not to damage the oil pan gasket.

![](images/8c5c67976747aa10394cbcf688e1461f683096b4b98d88c2e1b2df48cb7eeb20.jpg)

4. Install the inner oil seal assembler to the crankshaft flange.

Oil Seal Assembler 601 589 03 43 00

![](images/805d01473db60615e04819b79926b765b5f9d03bd3570f7ecbc53234b0506c2e.jpg)

5. Coat a little oil on the sealing lip of new radial seal and contacting surface.

### Notice

The sealing lip of the repair radial seal is offset to the inside by 3mm to ensure that it does not run in any groove which the standard radial seal may have left on the crankshaft flange.

A Standard Radial Seal B Repair Radial Seal

7. Install the outer oil seal assembler on he seal and by tightening the bolts, press the radial seal into the end cover as far as the stop.

### Notice

The seal must be positioned exactly at right angles in the end cover to ensure that it provides a proper seal.

Oil Seal Assembler 601 589 03 43 00

![](images/d4e13ff9ef1093fcc67e134794f09daff7c300d69393ac5c0f2b503b10c65db3.jpg)

![](images/7d73a6fbc0ddcb209d0f51f17be32c9af71736c944251ad40057c8cf40fe87e9.jpg)

### VIBRATION DAMPER AND HUB

Preceding Work Removal of the cooling fan Removal of poly V-belt

![](images/256d009870b8eae11b009f7ff27ceb65e77926b262540d548528f8e40d18ad68.jpg)

1 Bolt 10Nm
2 Cooling Fan Belt Pulley
3 Socket Bolt 23Nm
4 Crankshaft Belt Pulley
5 Vibration Damper
6 Bolt 200Nm + 90°
7 Washer
8 Straight Pin
9 Hub
10 Oil Pan

### Sectional View

![](images/db4dafc0217553d1e175cd2a747a3847777f7f6f4d870a189635bdd9a246a458.jpg)

3 Socket Bolt
4 Crankshaft Belt Pulley
5 Vibration Damper
6 Bolt
7 Washer
8 Straight Pin
9 Hub
11 Radial Seal
12 Timing Gear Case Cover
13 Key
14 Crankshaft Sprocket
16 Crankshaft

### Notice

The mounting position of vibration damper is fixed by straight pin (8).

Tools Required 602 589 00 40 00 Engine Lock 103 589 00 30 00 Puller

### Removal Procedure

1. Remove the starter motor and install the engine lock into the wheel ring gear.

Engine Lock 602 589 00 40 00

![](images/7247a26c26418684790d2a47841e5cc49440c8ba9bc3a525c26be260f2644cee.jpg)

2. Remove the poly V-belt.
3. Remove the cooling fan. Notice Keep the fan in vertical position.

![](images/636a2a868d8f861e848a1b793649fafcc11837af2489658e32b38ca36c9db810.jpg)

4. Remove the cooling fan belt pulley (2). 5. Place alignment marks (arrow) on the vibration damper (5) and crankshaft belt pulley (4).

6. Remove the timing sensor bracket. Notice Remove if necessary.

![](images/9d39e2710fc66fa9541020e9b4a1b64b16cd1d687d9b33ea7dee3c6b3d2550fe.jpg)

7. Remove the socket bolts (3) and then remove the belt pulley (4) and vibration damper (5).

8. Remove the washer and bolt.

![](images/05f5d3e334ba7062f5ed5eda6b50cdfb89129c8fb2d740f0de20dde2b658d609.jpg)

9. Remove the hub by using a puller.

10. Replace the radial seal.

Puller 103 589 00 33 00

### Installation Procedure

1. Install the hub. Notice Exactly align the woodruff key and the groove of hub (arrow).

![](images/431152ad345aabbd52f3bf24bb32ed861f47236bc3b628e4c6e1020110195231.jpg)

2. Install the washer (7) and tighten the bolt (6).

![](images/c33a826207e69ab2e88e0edd6786769a055310218ba7218d01caa33a7c2aa66e.jpg)

![](images/ebe5920bfe6dc10c26210b28a9d090ca2b9854ed61dd94331234a517da846728.jpg)

3. Install the vibration damper.

### Notice

Exactly align and insert onto the straight pin.

![](images/916ef74db723ad233375e935cea3aeea5dad4a5e6e91a61cf41efc34118ab87d.jpg)

4. Install the belt pulley.

![](images/5d4f641be913cbb386303ca05318c6831a8595f990b49422d29568f33792bbb0.jpg)

### Notice

See the ‘TDC setting’.

![](images/515bb9244937f5a4d13bf24620dc2844dbd8e6cce56e782082bb152f4c86ff95.jpg)

6. Install the cooling fan pulley.

![](images/828e400079776ad3d2cb40e837ec8a7f8568b046b0680bb23c6b9521a4ffc477.jpg)

7. Install the cooling fan.
8. Install the fan belt.

![](images/9441eea80da94811aba1db4c8dc828988f2bf5e974a497c26ce64c253401b6a8.jpg)

9. Remove the engine lock.

### CRANKSHAFT FRONT RADIAL SEAL

![](images/5a52c416ee753d985063a79dcd922cacc1fe6f08359d491b85e3d3c8aafc1f77.jpg)

1 Radial Seal
2 Timing Case Cover

3 Woodruff Key

Tools Required 601 589 03 14 00 Sleeve

### Notice

The sealing lip of the repair radial seal is offset to the inside by 2mm to ensure that is does not run in any groove which the standard radial seal may have left on the crankshaft flange.

A Standard Radial Seal B Repair Radial Seal

![](images/206af5219870ed4730db723e08494cbf7f9234a83127cf22341b0b67871e43b5.jpg)

### Replacement Procedure

1. Pull out the radial seal (1) and be careful not to damage the sealing surface of timing case cover. 2. Thoroughly clean the mounting bore of the radial seal.

![](images/2e16e29b30a9e1c430eb8d2ce76d5f2db6a961f1dfdaece34344d2929dddf8c9.jpg)

3. Coat a little oil on the sealing lip of new radial (1) and contact surface.

### Notice

Align the groove of sleeve and woodruff key(arrow).

![](images/2bcf9eaa6a985712ef53a27dcaab86bfdab981411835a1e02254ce47a2619baf.jpg)

Manual transmission only.

1. Remove the manual transmission.
2. Using a puller, pull out the locking ring and ball bearing together.

Counter Support 000 589 33 33 00
Internal Extractor 000 589 25 33 00

3. Apply Loctite 241 on the new ball bearing and then insert the ball bearing to be stopped at the spacer ring by using a proper mandrel.

### Bearing Clearances

mm
Matching Fit Bearing Journal Width to Thrust Bearings
![](images/130dbbecc05b1a40aca7b914a701a7a8ef071944ce8b492624811853520b5514.jpg)

mm
![](images/fc5392892b7d569e20aeb23f353c6b7e8b0d84848075dae7e87c22e39342d465.jpg)

Measure crankshaft axial clearance and adjust with proper thrust Bearing.
The same thickness of washer must be installed on both sides of the fit bearing.

Matching Crankshaft Bearing Shells to Basic Bearing Bore in Crankshaft
![](images/e14cd6ea3063327388e5c62f08676c00daab140e6f841b1a639a9a08bbf621bd.jpg)

![](images/29e8afcb3bd20e1b978e3fa4a0ffb6ae6acdd5aff151a37291fa0e7612bc8e8a.jpg)

### Notice

The crankshaft bearing caps are marked with stamped numbers. Remove the bearing cap from the vibration damper side.

![](images/26fb2d8391880595a6ad583cf6d9421471781666053a85f9670e7c9f47f54841.jpg)

3. Remove the crankshaft bearing caps (9) and lower thrust bearings (7).
4. Remove the lower thrust bearings (6) from the bearing cap (9).

![](images/6f67b36b98c7d0caa4bdcb8e9cf0c1b9c8ed73b9756ef9724664d0d2baa70926.jpg)

5. Remove the crankshaft (5).

6. Remove the upper thrust bearings(4).
7. Remove the upper bearing shells (3) from crankcase.

![](images/2a0b50525af5d0a181cbee93ee51d1198ccd6227652bb961e4e87edf1c1ef552.jpg)

8. Thoroughly clean the oil gallery.

9. Select a proper new bearing shells with reference to table.

10. Coat the new bearing shells with oil and insert into the crankcase and into the crankshaft bearing caps.

### Notice

Do not mix up upper and lower crankshaft bearing shells.

11. Install the bearing caps according to marking and tighten the 12-sided stretch bolts.

![](images/e2bc70685742396b1be5b5d7032a133646d82f3f8a82a6954e0df3648f04f184.jpg)

### Notice

No. 1 is vibration damper side.

12. Measure crankshaft bearing diameters (E).

![](images/e5d2361d10bfc707e4b5e98ac2e0d2b962c2c075230308c2be1f1c18d5cfbca0.jpg)

Extension 366 589 00 21 05

13. Measure at 3 points (A, B and C) and if the average value of B and C is less than A’s value, the average value of B and C is the mean value and if more than A’s value, A’s value is the mean value.

![](images/b82a578d51b5f233bec28ea8e33d0f778a5d0f6be287c10c562a3b7f78a7f35c.jpg)

14. Measure crankshaft bearing journal diameter (F).

When measured in A and B, the runout should not exceed 0.010mm.

-nUuA

### Notice

15. Measure radial clearance of crankshaft bearing (G).

![](images/32ec13fca19e850ac09560283904dd30e892144941a0dfd06e4257b69f99824c.jpg)

### Notice

If ‘G’ is out of standard, replace the bearing shells and adjust the radial clearance of crankshaft bearing.

Example) Measured value ‘E’ = 57.700mm Measured value ‘F’ = 57.659mm Clearance ‘G’ 0.041mm

16. Remove the crankshaft bearing cap.

17. Measure width of thrust bearing journal (H) and adjust with proper thrust bearings (see table).

### Notice

The same thickness of thrust washers should be installed on both sides of the thrust bearing.

18. Coat the upper thrust bearing (4) with oil and insert into the crankcase so that the oil grooves are facing the crank webs (arrow).

![](images/1153acccbb5520dd124bd1bb24079a944baa282c45cb668a7e3a7764c858585f.jpg)

19. Coat the lower thrust bearing (7) with oil and insert into the crankshaft bearing cap so that the oil grooves are facing the crank webs (arrow).

### Notice

The retaining lugs should be positioned in the grooves (arrow).

![](images/fc4f56cadfeefb7a9aa6339e45b06600e3c679c573d6af45176119a680e3f2d9.jpg)

### Notice

If he max. length of bolts(L) exceed 63.8mm, replace them.

![](images/4440647597cec38bbba4f1a4047f3994eaead539b1c67539278f1b7820fbe2e9.jpg)

19. Coat the new crankshaft with engine oil and place it on the crankcase.

20. Install the crankshaft bearing caps according to marking and tighten the bolts.

![](images/f9b53a0b4e1de0d1b7c69f8f786b091b32148903548506d4ae20a9a8d3648628.jpg)

### Notice

Install from No. 1 cap.

23. Measure crankshaft bearing axial clearance.

![](images/3edd913ee14b7c120ecc08ea666422a1712edc7024df227fd92c65cd7ed29f6e.jpg)

### Notice

If the clearance is out of standard, adjust the axial clearance of crankshaft bearing by replacing the thrust washers.

22. Rotate the crankshaft with hand and check whether it rotates smoothly.

### Notice

Dial Gauge 001 589 53 21 00
Dial Gauge Holder 363 589 02 21 00

The same thickness of thrust washers should be installed on both sides of the thrust bearing.

![](images/a9f9e8f87aef420b2e88305b8369ed612a1c66c79b591ce7c0cc99bf049bc16f.jpg)

24. Insert the new connecting rod bearing shells into the connecting rod and connecting rod bearing cap and tighten the 12-sided stretch bolts (11).

![](images/e5a4a952714ab0e7775d28a4960a24e720711bf48075a3bed049fbbdb12d0a29.jpg)

25. Measure inner diameter of connecting rod bearing.

![](images/62e85df891c8d53d27ca4e23d8d75eb961e3d246f9a3dbe56a56ea3bf8b464b2.jpg)

26. Measure connecting rod bearing journal diameter (K).

### Notice

Refer to measurement of the crankshaft bearing journal diameter.

27. Measure the radial clearance (L) of the connecting rod bearing.

Example) Measured value ‘J’ = 47.700mm Measured value ‘K’ = 47.653mm Clearance ‘L’ 0.047mm

![](images/935f7b3875258a2284869b289368854f98c95c7f7cd5782badb0db9fa0c20d63.jpg)

![](images/08a79fddcf1e5cbb643ef5fc734bb04c68ea9b96c4271de14c1b7eb39165586e.jpg)

### Notice

If the clearance is out of standard, adjust the radial clearance of connecting rod bearing by replacing the connecting rod bearing shells.

28. Remove the connecting rod bearing cap.

29. Install the piston.

30. Rotate the crankshaft by hand and check whether it rotates smoothly.

31. If the bearings are damaged, replace the oil presser relief valve. clean the oil pump and oil filter housing carefully and replace the hose if necessary.

### Notice

After assembling the engine, check the camshaft timing, adjust the start of fuel injection and check the TDC sensor bracket setting.

32. Fill oil and run the engine and then check the oil pressure and oil level.

### Notice

Install the original oil filter element and then change the engine oil and oil filter element after 1,000 1,500km.

### FLYWHEEL

Preceding Work Removal of the transmission Removal of the clutch

### Removal & Installation Procedure

1. Install the engine lock.

Engine Lock 602 589 02 40 00

![](images/90ab3ca2f47d13a38da650ad7b9470136ac9085d9542d784d2fd30cf1d3f2fcf.jpg)

2. Remove the 12-sided stretch bolts (4).

Installation Notice

![](images/ba7bb5c2c1f3fd4ef19930f6e4347bad27d70164495696c6a1a6928f6c0fc066.jpg)

![](images/6cc13112c9abe0ea99797fdc5fbcefb14a7f0ba32eb374c6f4a9c36c26434eb8.jpg)

3. Remove the flywheel (3), if equipped with manual transmission.

![](images/8ff80af428d40a8c985c9404e383edd99b438f19fa887e6daacdc014c3202389.jpg)

4. Remove the flywheel (6) and driven plate (5), if equipped with automatic transmission. Installation Notice Correctly align the position of dowel pin (2).
5. Installation should follow the removal procedure in the reverse order.

![](images/cbd7d68e15cbd28078c3eec99aa6668efa4c203c8ed2fcc23fb49348a87f3ab2.jpg)

### Notice

Flywheels which have scorch marks, scoring or cracks in the clutch surface should be machined by grinding or precision-turning. If the scores or cracks are severe than permissible specifications, replace the flywheel.

![](images/95fc2ae70d0a5446cf1aab68d519cf8048f93877f40d3d033ebff94e568c2f05.jpg)

When machining the clutch surface ‘A’, the mounting surface (B) for the clutch pressure plate should also be machined in accordance with ‘A’ to keep the distance ‘a’. Do not machine under ‘b’ value.
When machining, fix the flywheel exactly not to exceed the standard runout.

![](images/e73af5741ee8906eec9a2a8d202c910fd8166e0155e9a0a06ea8fb8af8595baf.jpg)

### FLYWHEEL RING GEAR

Preceding Work Removal of flywheel

![](images/e82a55c4dab3e99f651f4aa00dd762a1495073c588ad3613c0fb97dfe24c6b99.jpg)

1 Ring Gear
2 Flywheel

Tools Required 001 589 53 21 00 Dial Gauge 363 589 02 21 00 Dial Gauge Holder

### Replacement Procedure

1. Drill a hole into the ring gear (1) (arrow) and snap with a chisel. 2. Thoroughly clean the collar surfaces of ring gear.

3. Measure diameter (a) of centering collar.

![](images/80a83c2b5d89fa33b82dcdeef2bc5690f92dad38a0dd7aceee551616ecfcd858.jpg)

### Notice

If out of standard, replace the flywheel.

4. Heat up the new ring gear up to 220°C by using a heating device.

### Notice

Use temperature measuring chalk.

5. Install the new ring gear (1) onto the flywheel by using a drift.

![](images/813570df8a73b364fef578f76e97e066e458e345d02d89f98106982ae5896988.jpg)

![](images/d8965927069b0f9ea59691ff6d2f876522f8a6403da0573f716cd2505221363b.jpg)

6. Measure axial runout of ring gear (1) on a surface plate.

![](images/19a5fe91a251d113301aa0fbdce7219750d06a251636a59b188fa97bdd85baf5.jpg)

### Notice

For correct measurement, put the flywheel on the flat measuring board.

Dial Gauge 001 589 53 21 00
Dial Gauge Holder 363 589 02 21 00

![](images/e235cd201425bf8e3b5f7784bca7da650f0ec4a05f901aed50365b83239f4327.jpg)

### HYDRAULIC VALVE CLEARANCE COMPENSATION ELEMENT CHECK

![](images/9aa0ce51a92982cc07050d4d1876a378ff3be27cbf122bfe064e65b493747395.jpg)

1 Cylinder Head Cover 4 Valve Tappet
2 Bolt 10Nm 5 Cylinder Head
3 Gasket Replace

### Notice

The noise which continues short time during short travel (frequent starting of the engine) or engine starting after a long time storage is normal operating conditions. So, it does not need to be repaired. Determine the malfunctions in valve clearance compensation device with noise through following tests. If defective, replace as respectively.

1. Run the engine at more than 3000rpm for approx. 4 minutes.

2. Stop the engine. After 5minutes, check the engine oil level and adjust if necessary.

3. Remove the cylinder head cover.

4. Check the valve tappets at TDC position of each cylinders.

5. Using a drift, lightly press the valve tappet and measure clearance between the cam and valve tappet.

### Notice

If the clearance exceeds 0.4mm, replace the valve tappet.

6. If a valve tappet moves down too far in comparison to the others, replace the valve tappet.

7. Rotate the engine and check the remaining valve tappets.

### Notice

Unnecessary rotation of the engine will damage the valve tappets. Do not rotate the engine by using the camshaft sprocket bolt or to the opposite direction of the engine rotation.

![](images/f63ff8b4289b2c5bca59a8c89d00406c9f0f75ed8748ff5e0276de1eaba2de6f.jpg)

### VALVE TAPPETS

Preceding Work Removal of camshaft

![](images/3dc034ec5949d7a2ce535b3617e884fff58e05dbe739ce44932892b22a33c1be.jpg)

1 Valve Tappet 23 Cylinder Head Oil Gallery

4 Seal Replace
5 Screw Plug

### Replacement Procedure

1. Pull out the valve tappet (1).

Magnetic Bar 102 589 03 40 00

![](images/ce8d42cbbce330ddfadd0e10b43a5aed8860b21824494115ca49f209f356da96.jpg)

2. Remove the plug (5) and blow compressed air into the oil gallery (3). At this time, check that the outlet bores(arrow) at the seat of the valve tappet are clear. 3. Replace the seal (4) and tighten the plug (5). 4. Insert the new valve tappet.

### Notice

Coat the valve tappet with oil.

![](images/41d9dc31808a7ae738fb15ebe10cc92779d86c15a11ddd4d1799b7e42013c84e.jpg)

Check the stud bolt (6) for damage and replace necessary.

![](images/b42ef7ae8ccaac0ee388deb54fb61c03793ea59e45bab8be09923cd7c214b981.jpg)

2. Install the assembling board (11) to the cylinder head with 4 cylinder head blots (10).

Assembling Board 601 589 01 59 00

![](images/9358c93564b3e2fc10759cb134a6b3719ffd0dad032e9fd796003ca4da311597.jpg)

![](images/4035cf571d1b1ea34d0791dcac9999f905a2e90e15ee2c034d2b232cc53b2804.jpg)

![](images/3500d314893f90d9a25613ef7f1cbef06978a031558ced3fe982be40bf9be156.jpg)

Supporting Bridge 601 589 02 59 00

：n y

5. Using the press lever (14), press the spring retainer downward and remove the valve cotters (2) with magnetic finger (15).

### Notice

Press Lever 667 589 00 31 00

![](images/419319a282ccc25452342cd3c04223d8a7667b4c2a6fc116c75987d0fc7fb4ab.jpg)

### VALVE SPRINGS (CYLINDER HEAD INSTALLED)

### Removal & Installation Procedure

1. Remove the valve tappet (1) with magentic lifter. Notice Place the valve tappets upside down (open end upward)

Magentic Liter 102 589 03 40 00

![](images/0e8ef9b2b9818fac6dba3f8a220bb5ee6607186d84f0fbf7e3b90c001586bd05.jpg)

2. Install the holding wheel (7) into the timing chain of camshaft sprocket piston.

Holding Wheel 603 589 01 40 00

3. Position the piston of relevant cylinder at TDC.

![](images/d804b1707b65a85194657dbe6d0dc4929385de259f2911cecbdf11ac3003c29a.jpg)

4. Install the supporting bar (8).

Supporting Bar 667 589 02 63 00

![](images/a5aefadc6b41cb86867830abfbff4ffdfabf1fd06c187043e2df9c885c5333d5.jpg)

5. Using the press lever (9), press the spring retainer(3) downward and remove the valve cotters (2) with magnetic finger (10).

### Notice

Be careful not to damage guide bore of the valve tappet.

Press Lever 667 589 00 31 00
Magnetic Finger 116 589 06 63 00

![](images/9d3ef8db58f9e6ddd53603a1cf15e0d918a84e9af32cbb21311375bf40b0ee3b.jpg)

6. Remove the spring retainer(3) and spring (4). 7. Check the valve spring and replace if necessary. 8. Insert valve spring (4) with the color coding (arrow) facing down and insert valve spring Retainer(3).

9. By press the spring retainer(3) with press lever (90), install the valve cotters with magnetic finger (10).

Press Lever 667 589 00 31 00
Magnetic Finger 116 589 06 63 00
10. Remove the supporting bar.
11. Remove the holding wheel (7) from the timing chain (5).

![](images/13cbd845a89e0628a91a91715979af1deefac8745659cc4abfef28be8a17d6d1.jpg)

Holding Wheel 603 589 01 40 00

![](images/af7bafe5edccab00164b7629196e5d118c1b1eb911334c18b6d82350d3d42f55.jpg)

13. Coat the valve tappet with oil and install it.

Magnetic Bar 102 589 03 40 00

### Tools Required

### Replacement Procedure

1. Remove the valve tappet (1) with magnetic lifter. Notice Place the valve tappets upside down (open end upward).

Magnetic Lifter 102 589 03 40 00

![](images/35608fe06c1aaebfa004f6c3c98300eb24c91d1dd5afeec59a9abe4e22b6e17c.jpg)

Holding Wheel 603 589 01 40 00

3. Position the piston of relevant cylinder at TDC.

![](images/26e76653b14e20f9201cfece52733b9de91bac2e4fd21aafb9a746b66d5d56b8.jpg)

Supporting Bar 667 589 02 63 00

![](images/a03d2c29176f3e88b5b5df03f7be927ceeffb9c6533e51875adfc0b36c3b931f.jpg)

5. Using press lever (9), press the spring retainer(3) downward and remove the valve collets with magnetic finger (10).

Press Lever 667 589 00 31 00
Magnetic Finger 116 589 06 63 00

![](images/da60492acf51abf833ed39bb77671386323bede77e2bec872ccd750780863950.jpg)

Pliers 104 589 00 37 00

![](images/af45e2562f92b986403aff6d9b18b35c5b3479a44e88c33e2dbf2c250b7b8931.jpg)

8. Insert the cap (12) onto the valve (6) and install the new valve stem seal (5) and then remover the cap.

Drift 601 589 02 43 00

![](images/d0e934a93fb28e88d90e91245e55ea21e3e266869b20466f9be3a86b9f873d78.jpg)

### Notice

Be careful not to damage guide bore of the valve tappet.

Press Lever 667 589 00 31 00

### CHECK AND REPLACEMENT OF VALVE GUIDES

Preceding Work Removal of cylinder head Removal of valve spring Removal of valve

![](images/922799433c4c016a23bb0353e261d888b2d43936866024f655f984d8a7bad266.jpg)

1 Cylinder Head
2 Valve Guide

3 Valve Seat Ring a Basic Bore Diameter

### Notice

Measure center (arrow) of the valve guide and if the inner diameter ‘A’ exceeds standard value, replace the guide.

![](images/c4a50b5a8f2f66b5de4148db756c5b2404bf1a84ba029d5a4c571cd7778bad79.jpg)

### Tools Required

000 589 10 53 00 Reamer (for Exhaust)
000 589 10 68 00 Cylinder Brush
000 589 21 53 00 Reamer (for Intake)
102 589 00 23 00 GO / NO GO Gauge (for Intake)
103 589 02 15 00 Drift (for Exhaust)
103 589 03 15 00 Drift (for Intake)
117 589 03 25 00 GO NO GO Gauge (for Exhaust)
346 589 00 63 00 Super Cooling Box
601 589 02 23 00 GO/NO GO Gauge
601 589 05 15 00 Drift (for Intake)
601 589 06 15 00 Drift (for Exhaust)

### Checking

1. Thoroughly clean the valve guide bore using a cylinder brush.

Cylinder Brush 000 589 10 68 00

![](images/ac75dbb972f609531d5898307b65db4b3ee2e5efaeaf0f0c3d3410e4ded308c4.jpg)

2. Insert the GO/NO GO gauge into the valve guide bore. If the NO GO side is inserted fully, replace the valve guide (Intake 8mm, Exhaust 9mm).

GO/NO GO Gauge 601 589 02 23 00

![](images/aa93bc309afdaeb4016ec73b5cba0921616d0fa666a4b58435dd5ad788cfc47e.jpg)

### Notice

The valve guide must be driven out upward of the cylinder head.

Drift (for Intake) 103 589 03 15 00
Drift (for Exhaust) 103 589 02 15 00

2. Thoroughly clean the basic bore by using a cylinder brush.

Cylinder Brush 000 589 10 68 00

3. Check the basic bore in cylinder head for scoring marks and ream to next repair size if necessary.

![](images/47bac1e514cd7dbad948586d722f571ad30894989e8874515cfd96969fb89ecd.jpg)

4. Reaming basic bore in cylinder head (repair size).

Thoroughly remove carbon deposits in cylinder head. Notice Particularly remove the insides of the valve seat rings.

Remove the elevation (arrow) of intake valve seat rings.

![](images/f189b5af7c3267ef7e56e98e4dab99446df5d53d008cdf45f77fc7fb2a0dd6fa.jpg)

Select correct broaching tool and guide sleeve (refer to the table).

### Notice

Before broaching work, the broaching tool must be cleared of swarf with a stiff plastic brush.

Lubricate the basic bore, guide sleeve and broaching tool with petroleum. Push broaching tool (6) in broaching direction (arrow) into the guide sleeve (7) far enough so that the first cut of the broaching tool is positioned in the basic bore when guide sleeve is fitted onto the valve seat ring (3).

6. Broaching tool
7. Guide sleeve

See the ‘standard data’

![](images/204f8b01fd1b09f4bc17781239dd0594361b603c2e935e0a165786766fd38783.jpg)

Center the guide sleeve (7) in the valve seat ring (3) by turning.
Knock through the broaching tool (6) with a plastic hammer (approx. 25g). and aluminum drift.

![](images/1c16e29d59db84ac76e2defd2a7e5b8e8c784d5f1ee0225132838d024904d7d1.jpg)

5. Heat the cylinder head (1) in a wear tank to approx. 80°C.

6. Cool down the new valve guide (2) with liquid nitrogen.

### Notice

![](images/d54dfb1b5d53b0774a6e8f38fc4407861ed5465e3eba36de1241922b19a4520d.jpg)

Do not touch the cooled valve guide by hand.

Super Cooling box 346 589 00 63 00

7. Drive in new valve guide with drift (8) until the wire ring makes contact.

### Notice

The valve guide must be driven in from the cylinder head cover.

Drift (for Intake) 601 589 05 15 00
Drift (for Exhaust) 601 589 06 15 00

![](images/733357841dbca0db517f5bd203a20b37da4f8fd5cd3f710d25b8a445dea6eb54.jpg)

8. Check the valve guide bore with GO NO GO gauge (9). The GO side (marked ‘0’) should just still drop. If the GO side cannot be inserted, the bore of valve guide should be reamed.

### Notice

Perform the check only on cooled down cylinder head.

GO / NO GO Gauge (for Intake) 102 589 00 23 00
GO / NO GO Gauge (for Exhaust) 117 589 03 23 00

![](images/aff21d20617fbf956ff6e25fe2c6d8ee055f756fae64458c75a977a471f59750.jpg)

9. If necessary, ream the valve guide bore evenly.

### Notice

Never turn the reamer against the direction of rotation.

Reamer (for Exhaust) 000 589 10 53 00
Reamer (for Intake) 000 589 21 53 00

![](images/af422211ea179dbfcf894eb311b692d51b32ed4c321fb621c7d980344f1499ae.jpg)

### VALVE SEAT RINGS

Preceding Work Removal of valve Checking of valve guide, replace if necessary Removal of prechamber

![](images/c61ef9b4061468fd8c05cd4c13ef727592afa44d2cdcac547a74a19cdc41f44e.jpg)

1 Cylinder Head
2 Valve Seat Ring
3 Valve Guide
A Height (Cylinder Head Upper / Lower Surface)
B Height (cylinder Head Cover Surface Seat of Valve Seat Ring)

D Valve Seat Ring Outer Diameter D1 Valve Seat Ring Inner Diameter D2 Basic Bore Diameter H Height of Valve Seat Ring

### Removal Procedure

1. Measure dimension ‘A’.

![](images/d56e41ca27083dc67c6242aae64c11ed9e5c2baaa7ec27a226233aa3c02018d1.jpg)

2. Clamp the cylinder head with clamping device.

![](images/ee80dd323227b9907ef7af4bf6acaf580387803516fcdbc74691d6c930e534ba.jpg)

3. Cut groove into the valve seat ring so that dimension ‘C’ is approx. 2mm and dimension ‘E’ is approx. 6mm.

![](images/f0e73c011890d5d07b0bd7b125d2a7972abe058ec708c811ca13ccdad9cc8c77.jpg)

4. Remove the cylinder head from the clamping device and place it onto wooden blocks.
5. Remove the valve guide (3).

Drift (Intake) 8mm Drift (Exhaust) 9mm

![](images/7faebf8d24d8056750d806c036609a80872ce8b2044fce17b26ae4888f3f1664.jpg)

6. Insert the tensioning head (4) and extracted wedges(arrow) by turning the bolt (5).

### Notice

Carefully tighten the bolt (5) otherwise the valve seat ring in the cylinder head will be excessively tensioned.

7. Lock the bolt (5) with nut (6).

![](images/d2ef8c37ab89ab9916fd23f56d976be0ddc276fb74ef65b22486f7b3199a5ed6.jpg)

8. Turn over the cylinder head.
9. Remove the tensioning head (4) and valve seat ring (2) with drift (14mm) and removal tool.
10. Clean the basic bore of valve seat ring.

![](images/843d60b5e6d6db61be92af17ce2e2f1d382a71a49bf80a745b7fc0a3b01f2afa.jpg)

11. Measure the basic bore diameter (D2) and outer diameter (D) of the new valve seat ring (standard size).

12. Calculate the overlap value ‘U’ (D D2).

![](images/d33e95090d063d71dafbd6ac70a6eb791ed1fb39c3615fb7fcdb264823be7636.jpg)

![](images/cbba6a1148e31c816a25377002feabf5eb79424a327e874c5cc44fc391e3de7e.jpg)

13. Clamp the cylinder head with clamping device.

14. Machining basic bore for valve seat ring (repair size).

### Notice

Maintain minimum value of ‘B’.
Machine the basic bore. Measure machined basic bore and outer diameter ‘D’ of valve seat ring (repair size).
Measure the overlap ‘U’

Limit
![](images/7a6184a3aaf31cae811482bab26e69cb94634fe1c6cd778653509790458f7430.jpg)

![](images/9595b3107248a8700a2091367a70a276c87539d27de308a589f13fbb0c609ba6.jpg)

![](images/6cf2f6cd33fce992abfeb92ac311557b4cad5d00f287a1a118af9196902583a5.jpg)

![](images/96511c15c1ddd2361b149e3f512ec0997f10cd5efe0adeec3d01e67a2549ef83.jpg)

15. Hang the cylinder head (1) to the lifting device and heat in a water tank to approx. 80°C.

![](images/5eb01ea845eaf4da8a35e3239f5d2a99955111f75b99ea983a8807dcbeb28ab4.jpg)

16. Cool down new valve seat ring (2) into the cooling box with liquid nitrogen.

### Notice

Do not touch the cooled valve seat rings with hand.

Super Cooling Box 345 589 00 63 00

![](images/5f252dc981264ebbd3ba66111bbd429c6cd361fd8b3a04cf62a96c099201f050.jpg)

17. Drive in new valve seat ring (2) with a proper wooden drift.

![](images/537b65cf6ac1036549fe54a75faf75fbc4e367925a3b1bc9b50f134c3b5be85a.jpg)

18. Install the valve guide (3) with a proper drift and assembling tool.

### Notice

The valve guide must be driven in from the cylinder head cover.

19. Machine the valve seats.

Drift (Intake) 8mm Drift (Exhaust) 9mm

### CHECK AND MACHINING OF VALVES

Preceding Works Removal of cylinder head Removal of the vale spring Removal of the valve

![](images/8666fdda6523fe3c6256bb35f5b58ad0f0b82c88fed8a856d63c70c46f1d79eb.jpg)

I Valve Length W Camshaft Cam Basic Diameter X Distance (Camshaft Basic Bore Valve Stem)

Y Half Camshaft Basic Bore Diameter Z Distance (Cylinder Head Cover Parting Surface Valve Stem)

### Exhaust Valve

![](images/9bc02dff64af9c3fafd85a090914a6cc194821026b1edcd24c668705b94f4ced.jpg)

![](images/047fc26a57b7e9e2d4361a760bf39063a6b67476a7df461b710c16b9c8575385.jpg)

![](images/3ebeea94143f37521b9786687883c934355fbdf70edda51bdfa9d32b5162306d.jpg)

### Checking and Machining

1. Clean the valves and do visual check. Valves with wobbled valve disc, with worn or scored valve stem should be replaced.

2. Measure valve disc height ‘h’.

![](images/8457288137265c0fd7225f85aa17b5d24ffd19a77f14892f61b922a004eb74d2.jpg)

![](images/d4f1e89c447c773ea65e68600a1fb6bf2dc21290da195a593e4c963ad7966736.jpg)

3. Machine the valve.

### Notice

Pay attention to setting angle ‘a’.

![](images/2f345fe58d69bae3f9ffe3195803ce9dc2f2865af040106511e6327572372daf.jpg)

4. Measure radial runout between valve stem and valve seat.

![](images/65853bb3a044d9f7cf4b6dcd337adabf7ef19fee6fa73c27965ef5c8e6b4f728.jpg)

Dial Gauge 001 589 32 21 00

![](images/2ab009c03acab6d33e254534e87c9d9ebe7b4e8b4ee90a7519e0df0b0a5593e1.jpg)

5. Clean the vales, valve seats and valve guides.
6. Coat the valve stem with oil and insert it into valve guide.

![](images/63bda77ab12c55c5fc449540deeb5353489ec64b64bddf600440d743f52ebdcc.jpg)

7. Insert the valves (1) into the valve guides according to marking.

8. Measure amount by which the valve arrears ‘a’.

![](images/ff519b4719e07604c91bb4edf50e2149927695b1c838105e60369482b268c58d.jpg)

### Notice

If out of standard, replace the valve seat ring.

![](images/902e560e68a56bcccc54ea9fa7ed5b36f172871049169366ba3384c4e7766744.jpg)

9. Measure camshaft cam basic diameter (w).

![](images/4ba996895e5b70367d98978ca3bfc391284c6415b885dd30d1a6bccce551a69c.jpg)

![](images/fb63c7949623f0e24cd587fbf41a48278dd209f9c2c07197a18a6dd290d63ffe.jpg)

10. Measure distance ‘z’ (cylinder head cover parting surface valve stem).

![](images/3b49df666d61d00563b16cea8494ea2c7bf1d73ed8c93718e67975f9ffd00b2f.jpg)

11. The distance ‘x’ (camshaft basic bore valve stem). ‘x’ = ‘z’ - ‘y’.

Determine the valve to use according to this measurement (See ‘matching valves’)

Example) Measured value ‘w’ = 38.2 mm Measured value ‘z’ = 36.5 mm Value = 15.5 mm

‘x’ = 36.5 15.5 = 21.0mm

In this case according to ‘Matching valves’ table, the installed valve may be used.

### MACHINING OF VALVE SEAT

Preceding Work Removal of prechamber Removal and inspection of valve, replace if necessary Inspection of valve guide, replace if necessary

![](images/60eebc4faf8d10a96385cc06bde605a19b5e68fd6729d11440420cdf4b3bcc03.jpg)

1 Valve 234 Valve Seat Ring Cylinder Head Valve Guide

a Valve Seat Angle b Valve Seat Free Angle

### Machining Procedure

Valve machining is required

When the valve is leaking.
When replacing the valve.
When replacing the valve guide.
When replacing the valve seat or valve seat ring.

1. Machine the valve seat (a=45°).

![](images/ab0b8d2b4ada92d13223a9de20085b6ddecebf22c637198957c93d420446f287.jpg)

2. Measure valve seat width ‘b’.

![](images/0335d53f860c451035a13db3a8e6ddcf97d0495d5b6529b89fedea3c58cec9db.jpg)

![](images/e4da4b99cb7a8c6c8752683e5b09efd41d9c88d923cd878752b6007008f4d658.jpg)

3. If the specification is exceeded, the valve seat width has to be corrected at the lower free angle of ‘b’=65°.

![](images/29eb92f79cee540507596294029b3b6578ed3e3c7b07ea35df4d900f618b6cd9.jpg)

4. Measure radial runout.

![](images/f85934e0446ab06914d03bebd9d6e3e3eef654e591582b0480130206ff29f871.jpg)

![](images/6221359ce289ee398b986006ffc149f5ab059898b2f1e6f2b2ff094830572e0e.jpg)

5. Insert the valve (1) into the valve guide according to marking and measure amount by which the valve arrears ‘a’.

![](images/63b262bd2e5b7e997cae84a18fc61af0b666226deb149ca5e74f9072eb24b9db.jpg)

![](images/9269cf3557acc7369fe1aed3ab44c15ed5236d657ab378ff852230a243fd1941.jpg)

6. Measure camshaft cam basic circle diameter (w).

![](images/d8885815ffcf693c893581cb38d435bceba0e6dfd89995cbdb40a0da1d4ef7c5.jpg)

![](images/abc5d1bc0e5ba762f81b0c6dd31f05be55ce211b847e654eaac5408096b6e82e.jpg)

7. Measure distance ‘z’ (cylinder head cover parting surface valve stem).

![](images/9732cce2835cb0e4bcc78d0d1da491afe79774f5a4d21deef55b883bfb79270b.jpg)

![](images/2b54ebd09d982c061adae45718601a36494e76e45137dead2019d56c87982e45.jpg)

I Length of Valve w Camshaft Cam Basic Circle Diameter x Distance (Camshaft basic bore valve stem)

y Half Camshaft Basic Bore Diameter z Distance (Cylinder head cover parting surface valve stem)

8. Measure ‘x’ (Camshaft basic bore valve stem). ‘x’ = ‘z’ - ‘y

9. Determine the valve to be used. (See Matching valves’) Example) Measured value ‘w’ = 38.2 mm Measured value ‘z’ = 36.5 mm Value ‘y’ = 15.5 mm ‘x’ = 36.5 - 15.5 = 21.0 mm

In the case of a camshaft cam basic circle diameter ‘w’ = 38.2mm and a distance ‘x’ = 21.0mm, the installed valve may be used.

### CAMSHAFT TIMING TEST

Preceding Work Removal of glow plug

![](images/e458dfdac8cd6e9095cd1258405ec86d08901ab815d225fc94ae729060e8a5a3.jpg)

1 Cylinder Head Cover 4 Valve Tappet 23 Bolt  · 10Nm 5 Cylinder Head Gasket .. Replace

### Timing

![](images/8aaac61d2c692bad22460137d8acc8838b162768543bc02e09d289d8c05d80a8.jpg)

«At 2mm of valve lifting stroke.

### Measurement Procedure

1. Remove the cylinder head cover.
2. Rotate the engine in the direction of engine rotation until the intake valve of NO. 3 cylinder is completely closed. The cam lobe faces up (arrow).

### Notice

Do not rotate the engine at the bolt of the crankshaft sprocket. Do not rotate the engine in the opposite direction of engine rotation. If do, this will cause serious measuring errors.

3. Install the dial gauge holder and dial gauge (7) with the extension (8) to the cylinder head and position the tracer pin (9) onto the valve tappet (intake valve of cylinder NO.1) with a preload of min. 3mm.

### Notice

The tracer pin should be positioned exactly vertical.

Dial gauge Holder 363 589 02 21 00
Dial Gauge 001 589 53 21 00
Extension 366 589 00 21 05

4. Set the dial gauge to ‘0’.

5. Rotate the engine further in direction of rotation until the dial gauge has moved back by 2mm (valve lift) to 1mm.

6. Check the timing.

![](images/71c7b47347d180ca66852869d3dd1e6816cd2f78df9a584ce4deaad216858e43.jpg)

### Notice

If timing is out standard, the camshaft should be checked for wear and the timing chain for stretch. If a difference of more than 4° exists, the timing chain should be replaced.

7. Replace the gasket and install the cylinder head cover.

![](images/1aa23cee3289da28fd8680a5891f05d60308036e7ab44e52b0f84710fb204392.jpg)

![](images/385ba81c5565852b8d74ac21f983b534e303b773f0f9477a41f25d6ca06d92ad.jpg)

![](images/193156363d2416f9a2e2e743c561a1cc51aed924d0373d633be78824ad215910.jpg)

![](images/71cbd743537b60538f47dc3b3e58f9a700d6ceb4ed307d58351e31307eec7650.jpg)

### CAMSHAFT

Preceding Work Removal of cylinder head cover

![](images/ada6bda71509560e606ad3a3a5877d8c87e8d0fcb4b744a489e0cd89b1633bdc.jpg)

1 Bolt 25Nm
2 Washer
3 Camshaft Bearing Cap
4 Camshaft
5 Dowel Pin
6 Locking Washer
7 Timing Chain
8 Cylinder Head
9 Oil Pan
10 Camshaft Sprocket
11 Washer
12 12-Sided Bolt (M11) Check, 25Nm + 90°
13 Gasket Replace
14 Chain Tensioner 80Nm

### Notice

Do not rotate the crankshaft in the opposite direction of engine rotation.

![](images/5d69f4494e534c69b3489b1124efc7740d6f3cc760a144378fcfd9c0778d9cbf.jpg)

In this position, the markings of the camshaft/camshaft bearing cap (arrow) must be aligned.

![](images/30a346ac1f70050d8f6af3f75adf3c94f85a067583d91be24880dd9b790cb51d.jpg)

2. Place alignment marks on the camshaft sprocket and timing chain.

![](images/532d27e62cfa4e87ac80b9a66efa70def95ed4e037282a730d0b8291c3206a37.jpg)

3. Remove the starter motor and install the engine lock.

Engine Lock 602 589 00 40 00

![](images/3418bffa1bac9e2fd31c777e010d6b50798fca07a7ec7504547e71b63047cdf1.jpg)

4. Remove the bolt and then remove the camshaft sprocket.

### Notice

During removal, be careful not to drop the sprocket with chain. Remove the chain carefully and then pull out the sprocket.

![](images/410afac8922e4250c0c88f6aa1bdc0d8e362730e542cf83cdfbd6ae506ea71f4.jpg)

5. Remove the chain tensioner.

6. Remove the camshaft bearing cap bolts according to the removal order.

### Notice

Remove the camshaft bearing cap bolts of 1(dark arrow) first and then remove the bolts of 2 (light arrow) one revolution in stages until the counter-pressure is released. In order to avoid damaging the camshaft, it is essential to adhere to the removal order for the camshaft bearing caps.

OM 662LA

![](images/d1ce763ede50346b3546dc647e3a89ebe1ff6c1ad0164688031191176123a848.jpg)

OM 661LA

7. Remove the camshaft bearing cap.
8. Pull off the camshaft.

### Notice

Be careful not to miss front locking washer.

![](images/53138920f31369a06d58af59340356eb0b928c93570368e56d50eeedef0090b2.jpg)

9. Pull out the locking washer.

### Notice

Check the condition of locking washer and replace if necessary.

![](images/8905aff62dfa41484dcf3e0a7314cd5ad1bb49358078cc1ba9a40c81fa54c6f2.jpg)

4. Install the camshaft bearing caps according to markings(1, 2, 3 etc.).

### Installation Procedure

1. Insert the locking washer.
2. Check the valve tappet and ensure that tappet moves smoothly.
3. Coat the camshaft with oil and install the camshaft onto the cylinder head so that the TDC marking (arrow) is positioned upward vertically.

![](images/50a02e051e3e7bcbee2d55c134c51e76bdba70cd0ba0f372b7a0a0d8c5cf4cf1.jpg)

5. Tighten the camshaft bearing cap bolts acccording to installation order.

![](images/fa625beea8c97966c91ac0d5bb3f3109fc6f715938fbf83dbfbf9d6ffd75c4e6.jpg)

### Notice

Tighten the No. 1 bolts (light arrow) by one revolution in stages first and then tighten the No.2 bolts (dark arrow).

OM 662LA

![](images/0b7ebce48b817996f985d4f8bfc0f0596885824f699ba33747ecccf12a502373.jpg)

![](images/4f64de9a953f19281092d0fb34996ff02224902554f1e78d83d8427140f92400.jpg)

OM 661LA

6. Install the camshaft sprocket to be aligned the markings of the camshaft camshaft bearing cap (arrow).

### Notice

Align the alignment marks on the timing chain and sprocket.

![](images/ed8b48696e77567378e6672a349d9fa8ae91d003d40572c0d155faaf8c066153.jpg)

7. Tighten the camshaft sprocket bolt.

![](images/5f333583d747b1ef71ae67a7251665ea4a813cded86bb785b98d75669706c58c.jpg)

![](images/aaf0fee1bb0cfc7df4de1810b656f201853d1705cae4246f0296b1ff57708522.jpg)

### Notice

If max. length ‘L’ of the 12-sided bolt exceeds 53.6mm, replace it.

![](images/86b896c72b8c671dbcb3e98cb5301b1813a24c8c0c634d963980f79e9a4dbf0d.jpg)

8. Install the chain tensioner.

![](images/f2aac42720c53a36240013b38f4c163962816d152260d6a44ce4d33825e25a13.jpg)

### Notice

Replace the seal.
Before installation, by pumping in the oil approx. 10 times, fill the oil.

![](images/8e9181a4934cf4f69265ea78bd05dc39d4b7c5db276d37fcd54ab636a739aed5.jpg)

9. Remove the engine lock.

10. Check the TDC marking of OT on the crankshaft.

![](images/f93074017e964a568e32bd7c4d075dd4c68bee8addd9570716af5ecd32207882.jpg)

### Notice

Always install the chain tensioner to be filled with oil.

Place the chain tensioner in engine oil up to over the collar on the hexagon head with the thrust pin facing up. Slowly push down the thrust pin as far as the stop 7\~10 times with the aid of a press or a column drill

Faulty chain tensioners should be replaced completely.

![](images/232f70cf962502275f6c6816795ba8dfae2c54510178e42b0b7f660a76b5e396.jpg)

3 Screw Plug
4 Aluminum Gasket
5 Ball
6 Ball Guide
7 Compression Spring
8 Compression Spring
9 Housing

11 Valve Disc
12 O-Ring
13 Snap Ring

A Feed Bore from Cylinder Head B To Oil Pan

### TIMING CHAIN

Preceding Work Removal of glow plug Removal of chain tensioner Removal of cooling fan Removal of cylinder head cover

![](images/d262cb114c4a54271bb3e7be106090ffb3e8229984677271699398a01700906a.jpg)

1 Chain Link 234 Timing Chain Camshaft Sprocket Outer Plate

### Replacement Procedure

1. Cover over the chain box with cleaning rag and grind off both chain pins (arrow) at a chain link (1) of the timing chain.

![](images/ffe1934d3d352490b546e172622fb60166bda097ec6871a19dc530b13001ad1a.jpg)

2. Remove the outer plate (4) and chain link (1).

### Notice

Ensure that the ends of the timing chain do not drop into the chain box.

![](images/3d7344e603f4b0539e100584c05480d54e52eeb32f37cbe2205d4a1c088fb033.jpg)

3. Loosen the chain tension with approx. 4 turns. 4. Connect the new timing chain (arrow) with chain link (1) to the old timing chain (2).

![](images/8f83aeee272eed6c68f5e46c8cf42d935eb47e1b11a4e7fba5f5d572f663f958.jpg)

5. By rotating the crankshaft in the of engine rotation, pull out the old timing chain with installing the new timing chain. 6. Remove the old timing chain and hold the ends of the new timing chain to camshaft sprocket (3) with wire (arrow).

![](images/18f0ae7b93eca659a80051ed02b1185c6dcd87509fd847624ff5895307c6a409.jpg)

7. Insert the new chain link (1) to connect the chain.

![](images/4d0672055a84a5693df6af0969d291e891e117669449d58fafcdae96d10c0b24.jpg)

8. Insert the new outer plate (4) into the fitting tool (8) and then the outer plate will be held by a magnet.

Chain Assembling Device 000 589 58 43 00

![](images/df0f9f82c361426b1166c95330f28cd0f57aa28629f21c94e2e7d170a4c3a1c7.jpg)

9. Place the fitting tool (8) onto the chain link and press the outer plate on as far as the stop.

![](images/692fb54d137d89a63bdaaa5f2aaf95d2b1bdfc2c635a1fe2bbb79817786cee39.jpg)

10. Switch over the die (9) of the fitting tool (8).

11. Place the fitting tool (8) onto the chain link and rivet the chain pins one by one.

![](images/55a12ce08f1b9665103acd5fea865fcb036846a88239c3c2be8fed782723532d.jpg)

![](images/a3ff00ef9140f65f61e2a5df34ba90dc2bfcea3dac34035524919d5eadea0b99.jpg)

12. Check the riveting of chain pins and re-rivet if necessary.
13. Remove the wire.

![](images/416dd234a5cae42e2e65a3ea5674475270f571f5b442743a755c807f78287691.jpg)

14. Position the no.1 cylinder at TDC.

In his position, the marking on camshaft camshaft bearing cap (arrow) must also be aligned. If the markings are not aligned, the timing chain must be re-set and the injection pump timing has to be set.

![](images/7b902c673f8623fb9fa388667e912f043d6d9eb734f32a53c310bdf2701245ee.jpg)

### TENSIONING RAIL

Preceding Work Removal of cylinder head Removal of timing case cover

![](images/dbae5ac782b6601028f6f22267d77f1c7ed703c7594a72bd3c23052589be08c4.jpg)

1 Tensioning Rail

2 Bearing Pin

### CYLINDER HEAD GUIDE RAIL

Preceding Work Removal of the cooling fan Removal of poly V-belt Removal of chain tensioner

![](images/90af7ce9e70cce26fa750bb66ec0138cf46507ecc6288c0f43af9df6c954a5c3.jpg)

1 Cylinder Head Cover
2 Bolt 10Nm
3 Gasket Replace
4 Camshaft Sprocket
5 Washer
6 12-Sided Bolt (M11)   Inspect, 25Nm + 90°
7 Tensioning Lever
8 Bolt 23Nm
9 Bearing Pin Sealing Compound
10 Guide Rail
11 Bearing Pin
12 Guide Pulley
13 Socket Bolt 23Nm
14 Closing Cover
15 Spring
16 Timing Chain

### Removal Procedure

1. Removal the cylinder head cover.
2. Pull off the tensioning lever (7) and remove from the spring (15).

![](images/5d7642faef4023e66f42f0396cffeac9935eb00e6e91236b4002b646bc49c565.jpg)

3. Pry off the closing cover (14) and remove the bolt (13) and then remove the guide pulley (12).

![](images/f28a68851b0802eccec0d27f5f70aac0a3b996df1e9af438b338eea822536928.jpg)

4. Place alignment marks (arrow) on the timing chain (16) and camshaft sprocket (4).

![](images/adcef2a4d287fbb403a6aa57283de7c1c263a90cceb1f45fe41c010733b55043.jpg)

5. Remove the camshaft sprocket.

6. Pull out the bearing pins (9, 11) with sliding hammer and remove the guide rail 10.

Sliding Hammer 116 589 20 33 00
Threaded Pin 116 589 02 34 00

![](images/0ade8333ecf5076a13ed1c9cbd16e2d06c61ff459cfec9086b361d7827e760ba.jpg)

### Installation Procedure

1. Apply collar of both bearing pins with sealing compound.
2. Position the guide rail and insert the bearing pins.
3. Install the camshaft sprocket.

![](images/3101328294c56d4b619af4d4e60e485df7151b00145a070f5ff237025854465c.jpg)

![](images/0635d36516cd1f577148f50013b065d3436c7730b7f50fa1dd2c42049880957e.jpg)

### Notice

If the max. length ‘L’ of the 12-sided bolt exceeds 53.6mm, replace it.

![](images/1df50a4dbbee90492859e3575ffd5e1d578ee3e87bb4b9166d46c56385afa5ee.jpg)

4. Position the no. 1 cylinder at the TDC of OT.

![](images/da3cdfa14e7c4ef9d85b0eeb8310086043f64c760ac87b6387424790fc3ff5ab.jpg)

In this position, the marking on the camshaft camshaft bearing cap (arrow) must also be aligned.

5. Install the guide pulley (12).

![](images/0214c0d0522a65f734d21631365c85bea60f4d3be3a0e54a5ef4ab0953b403e8.jpg)

6. Attach the tensioning lever (7) to the spring (15) and install.

7. Replace the gasket and install the cylinder head cover.

![](images/6237378cddac4911353b943d54eb030925581e4bc355f075a8b2175814b27075.jpg)

![](images/d1a76dd522a594ed4fae75fcf0d03dda87d7364e915551d92b00d7cbabf5fc86.jpg)

### TIMING CASE COVER GUIDE RAIL

Preceding Work Removal of cylinder head cover

![](images/8c9d3d52b513404af35288b17cd9b376e0503b9cd8b98865cc81c7d280aad57b.jpg)

1 Bearing Pin
2 Bearing Pin
3 Guide Rail
4 Oil Pump Chain
5 Crankshaft
6 Spring
7 Bushing
8 Tensioning Lever

### Removal Procedure

1. Pull out the tensioning lever (8) together with the spring (6) and guide rail (3) far enough until the tensioning lever has moved passed the oil pump chain (4) and is resting against the crankshaft (5)

![](images/4164e525f4edcf33e45ce9014561facf7efa1653a2041bddd2e294c3d261481b.jpg)

2. Pull the tensioning lever (8) off the bearing pin (2) and carefully remove the spring (6). Remove the tensioning lever (8) together with the spring (6) and bushing (7).

![](images/6a653c53153f6db7cb1de52a6778ceaced3efee4f50608150d75d2c5602c9ffa.jpg)

3. Remove the guide rail (3).

![](images/3ee17afc754c6bf4c8badfe6f7f1b8190c97c78e23187ea313a3ec1a73816976.jpg)

### Installation Procedure

1. Install the guide rail (3). Attach the spring (6) to the guide rail and to the tensioning lever (8).

### Notice

Ensure that the spring is correctly located in the guide rail (arrow).

2. Push the guide rail, spring, bushing and tensioning lever together onto the bearing pin (1, 2).

![](images/489f43148cf5215578d51ffb2a81bfaea0e0601c4c4b6d793099bf49ad07b9fe.jpg)

### CRANKSHAFT SPROCKET

Preceding Work Removal of oil pan Removal of timing case cover

![](images/dac94d574a02c47117fd3258c6bbb14bf03d16ef4413c37c779bf7bbab76f1f0.jpg)

1 12-Sided Stretch Bolt (M1 ) .. Check, 25Nm + 90°
2 Washer
3 Camshaft Sprocket
4 Timing Chain
5 Bearing Pin
6 Bearing Pin
7 Oil Pump
8 Oil Pump Sprocket
9 Oil Pump Chain
10 Washer
11 Bolt 25Nm
12 Crankshaft Sprocket
13 Crankshaft
15 Key
16 Guide Rail
17 Spring
18 Bushing
19 Tensioning Lever

### Removal Procedure

1. Pull out the tensioning lever (19) together with the spring (17) and guide rail (16) far enough until the tensioning lever has passed the oil pump chain (9) and is resting against the crankshaft (13).

![](images/99e9a053e19d3f8e6da40c0e0610da8ef4d472c8b74278e8e282820f94a7540f.jpg)

2. Pull out tensioning lever (19), spring (17) and bushing (18) from the bearing pin (6).

3. Pull out the guide rail (16).

![](images/8259917f5434b12fd0780ffc0c1fe8030ac576bfcbc16a139fe3c42c60654c00.jpg)

4. Remove the bolt(11) and then remove the washer (10), oil pump chin (9) and sprocket (8).

5. Place alignment marks (arrow) on the timing chain (4) and crankshaft sprocket (12).
6. Remove the woodruff key(15).

![](images/fa268b1739958187cba28eb64a458e5f3b062ae8e61c85b39324edd2d560069a.jpg)

7. Place alignment marks (arrow) on the timing chain (4) and camshaft sprocket (3).

![](images/060edfd817cf978f2fd7bb510dd52b185a5edc347d4c8945b868175bfe8fc8f8.jpg)

8. Remove the chain tensioner.
9. Remove the bolt (1) and then remove the washer (2) and camshaft sprocket (3).

![](images/285ec43fd2991de1df1b65bc6a5e32fc4f77aa7c41e9cef0ad60cd9462de4eb6.jpg)

10. Remove the crankshaft sprocket (12) by using a puller (20).

Puller 102 589 05 33 00

![](images/8495c5556afe8ef48037eb8cc758ce20965e4cd7b884013908b41a8e110642ab.jpg)

### Notice

Check the timing chain, camshaft sprocket, injection pump timing sprocket, oil pump chain and oil pump sprocket for wear and replace if necessary.

![](images/05e3675bf338347e11695df362240f353915256624eaf534c3b74d3390eccd38.jpg)

2. Install the new crankshaft sprocket (12) by using a drift (21).

### Notice

Align the groove of sprocket and woodruff key (arrow) exactly. Drift 116 589 07 15 00

![](images/fe94993825926cb7707c59243809687a650f12d4bf891ae71a92e2ce61da2598.jpg)

3. Fit the timing chain (4) on the crankshaft sprocket (12).

### Notice

Align the alignment marks (arrow) on the chain and sprocket.

![](images/8aa2cc44ee0d690827cada6fa6db8646519ba0cfd911f9c8f26b7a6fb65e5efd.jpg)

4. Install the camshaft sprocket (3).

![](images/ddc6bd97092d6707743efb465a3ffab6b6a39e17cb09c4d5f2d6e73ba9f693e1.jpg)

### Notice

Pay attention on the alignment marks and dowel pin (arrow).

![](images/472beda7cb70e01a694373f603bf8ce13c028def17b9cc854b05299c2ee6e4fa.jpg)

### Notice

If the max. length ‘L’ of the stretch bolt exceeds 53.6mm, replace it.

![](images/fa41b5d5267199c39b7065f366753a526b25dcb6e031c707eb98839a1fe3d0a6.jpg)

5. Fit the oil pump chain (9) on the crankshaft sprocket (12) and insert the oil pump sprocket (8) into the oil pump chain and then install it on the oil pump.

### Notice

The curved side of the oil pump sprocket should face the oil pump.

6. Insert the woodruff key (15).

![](images/edb6007a0b7032d09871902afafad9bb9187111d7be37da237f915487a3c615a.jpg)

7. Install the guide rail (16). Attach the spring (17) to guide rail and to tensioning lever together onto the bearing pins (5,6).

### Notice

Ensure that the spring is correctly located in the guide rail (arrow).

![](images/8dfeddd728407b1735a6c1c2cc8f89ef3d3dd36f7b2cc6c31d5f57fba141565f.jpg)

14 Cylinder Bore

### Notice

There are two kinds of connecting rods (for machining) and they are different in weight approx. 15g. When replacing the connecting rod, measure its weight and ensure that a engine has the same weight of connecting rods. If not, there would be a unbalancing of engine.

### Removal Procedure

1. Remove combustion residues from the cylinder bores. 2. Remove the connecting rod bolts (12) and then remove the connecting rod bearing caps(11).

![](images/f9ea491cd4c9cb6b7334b58b80b32054883c830dba4af6f1e956beb75b54cfcd.jpg)

3. Remove the connecting rod bearing shells (9, 10).

### Notice

Be careful not mix the bearing caps and shells each other.

![](images/be16e77ed6858c0976b9c2488b183c16bb1945e543ff25abe1d5b527a11b1e44.jpg)

4. Remove the piston (6) and connecting rod (8).

![](images/59a06422a46c0a34e1553c261f1028271b43bab95bf8be7403f0b42b44ac416b.jpg)

5. Remove the snap ring (4) and pull out the piston pin (5).
6. Separate the piston and connecting rod.

![](images/50b6aa02bf81132f191931a9f96eb633055127a68386152a4cbda2e0224856f9.jpg)

### Installation Procedure

1. Fit the piston onto the connecting rod so that the arrow (15) and the locking slot (16) are facing in direction of the vehicle.

![](images/428c232c02fa416e4ddb020ee6d336fc89cd512c9b1b474c493de9cbfb75a22d.jpg)

2. Coat the piston pin (15) with engine oil and insert it by hand.

### Notice

Do not heat up the piston.

![](images/463521ae426734eb1f9ee41c33e35000d5e45ad031b6aa18813286c3719d1d6c.jpg)

3. Install the new snap ring (4) into the grooves (arrow). 4. Check the piston rings (1, 2, 3) and replace them if necessary.

![](images/c980fadbbeff9c52ca2bf059f7e12956a64ff51ac63f5b369399b935d1ed1768.jpg)

5. Install and arrange the piston rings to be evenly 120° from each ends gap.

![](images/30016f43639d803a61cf3d53ba0d45877db2bc7df026b6ab617329754d59a5f2.jpg)

6. Coat the cylinder bore, connecting rod bearing journal, connecting rod bearing shell and piston with oil. 7. Compress the piston rings with a tensioning strap (7).

Tensioning Strap 000 589 04 14 00

![](images/3e1972240f324923d5438ab69f24892217790243becec8e3e31787f456923d79.jpg)

8. Insert the piston assembly into the cylinder with a wooden stick (18).

### Notice

The arrow on the piston crown must point toward the front of vehicle.

![](images/acfe847b86bd7c648e132cd83062abb1e276c7798bd1c8c54fb80e6d04e0456e.jpg)

9. Insert the connecting rod bearing shells (9, 10).

### Notice

Be careful of the difference in upper and lower bearing shells and not to be changed.

10. Position the connecting rod bearings caps.

### Notice

Position so that the retaining lugs are on the same side of the connecting rod bearing(arrow).

![](images/29b47b1fedb44c0b158bc49694522919d0b2e7ac907b4c6ab69a974fe13079ea.jpg)

11. Measure stretch shaft diameter (C) of the connecting rod bolts.

![](images/0519c162b1aa609aa189189d644fe0e7d2fe63e8e01208bea539cf2668e25932.jpg)

![](images/187863173b75075465e7c59dc10611c33fe89ae3b4a4eb72be3a305720b5365a.jpg)

12. Coat the bolts (12) with oil and then tighten the bolts.

![](images/04608aa961cffa98bb5fc3eb2cbfe415a5ec525e04b3b4621422a6d7cdc64540.jpg)

13. Rotate the crankshaft and check axial clearance between the connecting rod and crankshaft.

![](images/3b1291a8de09830cfcc51dabbac04a23b493bf15de1ecaf458b1e9a39ad0914f.jpg)

14. Measure clearance between the piston crown and cylinder

![](images/c69c560893e17f42fe5be8245569acfd3dfa48efd6df562c8adf7cd15cebfa90.jpg)

![](images/38923595972dd781ff815a93738ccae93743a08e254525827db8792a785e56c3.jpg)

15. Position the piston at TDC and measure the distance between the piston crown and the crankcase surface.

![](images/0eb0c49596c8bf678c714d392e41df30d78797a81a2e54468f4925101a8ad675.jpg)

### Notice

Measure at points marked.

![](images/50428094d004f24649312719bb8245041b3f26cd64ac27e09fb77ebb542e8307.jpg)

### REPLACEMENT OF CRANKCASE CORE PLUG

![](images/db8011b9e3565fd6228486264005b7745a67f12da6aab72a156558574e32a55e.jpg)

![](images/a50d4ab7028f41d24acfbab9bf0829b3d3851f91d6a48ab11a41974380c41141.jpg)

Core plug f34mm OM661LA - 2EA OM662LA - 3EA

Core plug ： f34mm OM661LA - 2EA OM662LA - 3EA

![](images/b1bbee92b596e58112b37aa65fdab8d8b174cb0faef9b68ee079bc605dc8f843.jpg)

![](images/6a2750df7bd3b220d1aa2bd0174be3e0ddc675305de6f99813d6e4b9356e95b3.jpg)

Core plug f17mm OM661LA - 1EA OM662LA - 1EA

Core plug = f34mm OM661LA - 1EA OM662LA - 1EA

### Replacement Procedure

1. Completely drain the coolant. 2. Remove any parts which impede access. (Example transmission, injection pump) 3. Place the screwdriver to the deepdrawn edge of the core plug and pull forward and then rotate 90°.

4. Pull out the core plug with pliers.

![](images/6a050bbc9bd2f35de43fb7b27f374775289e66aabbb2e4b2732e0859f086405f.jpg)

![](images/37b5a4b5cd190822eaaa557d033b59bb73e4314369bee241c79a50c19c2fd558.jpg)

5. Thoroughly clean the sealing surface and apply Loctite 241.
6. Install the new core plug by using a drift.

Drift 102 589 00 15 00 (F34) Drift 102 589 12 15 00 (F17)

7. Install the removed parts and fill the coolant.

### Notice

The adhesive must be allowed to harden for about 45 minutes before filling of coolant.

8. Warm up the engine and check the coolant for leaks.

![](images/6e8be3ef02252951574a1e54aa61f09fa19a065db3b710e298bbbf15107bca31.jpg)
